// Panda_End_Game89_MidGame_Storage.txt  (v89 Mid Game, 4 Nov 2026: v88 + MakeMid v0.2 keeps - about half of End Game's sales or less) = Panda_End_Game86_MidGame_Storage.txt + three fixes from BOT17's first Auto run + the +16 bar (Make88_v0.1.py):
//   1. S tier at +8..+11 with a good substat 3x -> +12 (Battles8: kept)  2. Rare Merciless SPD x2 / CR% x2 kept
//   3. 5* Mythic S/A with SPD or CR% -> +12 before judging (Battles8: +8, kept to +11)
//   4. (Storage files) +16 judged with the +12 score bar (a dull last roll no longer sells a piece kept at +12)
// Panda_End_Game86_MidGame_Storage.txt  (v86, 31 Oct 2026): deep cleanse (Artifacts Storage, Accessories Storage)
// v86 = v85 + A tier at +12 and up sold unless kept by the v86 rules; Legendary / Mythic at +16 need one more good roll (bar +0.75)
// v85 = v84 with fewer upgrades to +8: the look at +8 only for 6* with SPD (damage sets: SPD or CR%%) + one more good substat; Mythic only 6* with two good substats (one SPD or CR%%)
// v84 = v83 without the rules that asked for 5 substats (RaidBot refuses them and drops the whole file; they could never match)
// Use: accounts without much S/A gear yet (Mid Game).
// = v82 quick cleanse + v83 keeps (score rule from Anh's reviews) + set accessory upgrade-and-check + Mythic judged like Legendary.
// Generated by MakeMid_v0.2.py (Make86 v0.5 + the Mid Game level: score bar 1.0, B sets like A tier, 5* to +8 for a look, more finished keeps).
//
// Panda_End_Game82_quick_cleanse_Storage.txt  (v82 quick cleanse, 23 Oct 2026; v81 had non-accessory sets in an accessory rule - RaidBot refused it)
// = Panda_End_Game80_Harsh_Storage.txt + Anh's 23 Oct corrections (first matching rule wins, so they sit right after the floors):
//   - Merciless accessories: keep all (like Mercurial)
//   - A tier accessories (Stone Skin, Slayer, Chronophage, Supersonic, Rebirth, Pinpoint), 5 or 6 stars, with a SPD substat: keep
//   - S and A tier artifacts with SPD boosted 2+ times: keep
//   Used on every farm account (Harsh base). Not for PandaMain.
//
// Panda_End_Game80_Harsh_Storage.txt  (v80)
// Storage file: "AutoSell rules file for In Box", "for Artifacts Storage", "for Accessories Storage".
// Panda End Game v80 - Harsh. Built from v69 + Tredepica v11.3 + reviews; see Panda_End_Game80_Summary.
// Use: every other account.
// SYNTAX: first matching rule wins. Keep / Sell / Upgrade n (levels the item, then all rules run again).
// Sub = SPD2 means SPD boosted 2+ times. Several Sub conditions in one rule never share a stat.
//
// BOOSTS by level (existing substats boosted first, missing ones revealed last):
//   Legendary +4:1 +8:2 +12:3 +16:4 | Epic +4:1 +8:2 +12:3 +16:3 (reveal) | Rare +4:1 +8:2 then reveals
// STANDARD = k good boosts, p of them on the first stat(s). Final level 12
// .
// An item is sold at the first level where it can't reach its standard any more; an item already at
// its standard is kept before any upgrade; upgrades jump to the next level where the check gets stricter.
//
// TIERS  S Mercurial, Merciless, Lethal, Zeal, Feral, Protection, Swift Parry, Deflection, Speed, Divine Speed
//        A Stone Skin, Shield, Savage, Cruel, Slayer, Reflex, Impulse, Provoke, Relentless, Chronophage,
//          Supersonic, Rebirth, Pinpoint, Bolster, Defiant
//        B Perception, Immortal, Stun, Cursed, Righteous, Regeneration, Stonecleaver
//        C everything else (incl. Guardian): SPD or CR% 3x (6* Legendary), or any substat 4x;
//          5* Mythic only with a 4x
//
// GOOD STATS (first stats in brackets)
//   speed sets      [SPD] HP% DEF% ACC RES       Pinpoint: ACC only via 3x       Shield/Stone Skin W/H/S: HP% required
//   damage sets     [SPD CR%] CD%; gauntlets: CR% main needs [CD%], CD% main needs [CR%]; ATK% not counted
//   Mercurial       [SPD] HP% DEF% ACC RES CR% CD%, or crit path [CR% CD%] + SPD
//   bulk            [HP% DEF%] RES SPD (Bolster, Defiant)
//   Speed, Divine Speed: SPD 2x     Feral: any substat 3x (boots as v69)
//
// STANDARDS (k good boosts / p first-stat boosts)
//   S 2/1   A 2/1   B 3/2 Legendary   C SPD/CR% 3x
//   SPD 3x on anything (6* Epic+, not flat boots): kept.  S/A: any substat 3x kept (ore keeps the boosts per slot).
//   Flat-main gauntlets/chests: SPD 3x (damage sets: SPD or CR% 3x).
// BOOTS  SPD main: good boosts on the other stats, S 2 / A 3 / B 3 / C CR% 3x.
//        HP%/DEF%/ATK% main: speed sets SPD 2x; damage sets SPD 2x or 2 crit;
//        Mercurial/Merciless 1 boost of SPD/CR%/CD% at 8.  Flat main: never upgraded; developed S Legendary with
//        one substat 4x kept for an ore.
// RARES  Mercurial/Merciless 5*/6* with SPD: Battles keeps, Storage takes to 8 and needs SPD rolled.

// ##############################################################################
// PROTECTED - before every floor
// ##############################################################################
Keep Accessory, Set = Swift Parry/Deflection/Feral/Mercurial/Merciless
Keep Accessory, Special = Reaction
Keep Accessory, Special = Bloodshield

// Floors
Sell, Rank < 5
Sell, Rarity < Rare
